STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)

Financing Act of 2025, for the

development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be

development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities

ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.

INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.
FOR ISSUE NO. 3
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 3
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.
FOR ISSUE NO. 3
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 3
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

BALLOT STYLE #33
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

COUNTY ISSUES
ISSUE INITIATED BY PETITION OF
THE PEOPLE
ISSUE NO. 5
LIBRARY MILLAGE
VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.
FOR ROAD TAX
AGAINST ROAD TAX

ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.

REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)

ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2

PRECINCT________________
BALLOT STYLE #38
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.
FOR ISSUE NO. 3
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 3
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

COUNTY ISSUES
ISSUE INITIATED BY PETITION OF
THE PEOPLE
ISSUE NO. 5
LIBRARY MILLAGE
VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.
FOR ROAD TAX
AGAINST ROAD TAX
Review

CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.

AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1

ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2

(OR OTHER MEASURE)

ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to

Within the Economic Development

(Ballot Title)

THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

COUNTY ISSUES
ISSUE INITIATED BY PETITION OF
THE PEOPLE
ISSUE NO. 5
LIBRARY MILLAGE
VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.

AGAINST ROAD TAX

VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.
FOR ROAD TAX
AGAINST ROAD TAX

INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.
FOR ISSUE NO. 3
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 3
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.
FOR ISSUE NO. 3
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 3
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

COUNTY ISSUES
ISSUE INITIATED BY PETITION OF
THE PEOPLE
ISSUE NO. 5
LIBRARY MILLAGE
VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.
FOR ROAD TAX
AGAINST ROAD TAX

CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

COUNTY ISSUES
ISSUE INITIATED BY PETITION OF
THE PEOPLE
ISSUE NO. 5
LIBRARY MILLAGE
VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY

(Popular Name)

Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.

GARLAND COUNTY, ARKANSAS - NOVEMBER 3, 2026
PRECINCT________________
BALLOT STYLE #50
INSTRUCTIONS TO VOTER
1. Vote by placing an appropriate mark ( ) opposite the person for whom you wish to vote.
2. Vote on amendments, acts, and measures by placing an appropriate mark ( ) below the amendment (or act or
measure) either FOR or AGAINST.
3. If you vote more than once in a contest, your vote in that contest will not count. If you make a mistake on your ballot,
return it to an election official for a replacement ballot. You may replace up to two ballots in an election.

STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 1
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to be
Known as "The Citizens Only Voting
Amendment" and Providing That Only
a Citizen of the United States Meeting
the Qualifications of an Elector Under
the Arkansas Constitution May Vote
in an Election in this State.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
CREATE "THE CITIZENS ONLY
VOTING AMENDMENT"; TO
PROVIDE THAT ONLY A CITIZEN
OF THE UNITED STATES MEETING
THE QUALIFICATIONS OF AN
ELECTOR UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION MAY VOTE IN AN
ELECTION IN THIS STATE; AND
PROVIDING THAT A PERSON WHO
DOES NOT MEET THE
QUALIFICATIONS OF AN ELECTOR
UNDER THE ARKANSAS
CONSTITUTION SHALL NOT BE
PERMITTED TO VOTE IN ANY
STATE OR LOCAL ELECTION
HELD IN THIS STATE.
FOR ISSUE NO. 1
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 1
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 2
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment to
Amend Arkansas Constitution, Article
2, § 5, to Protect the Right to Keep
and Bear Arms.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION TO
PROVIDE THAT THE CITIZENS OF
THE STATE OF ARKANSAS SHALL
HAVE THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS FOR THEIR COMMON
DEFENSE, FOR LAWFUL HUNTING
AND RECREATIONAL USE, AND
FOR ANY OTHER LAWFUL
PURPOSE; PROVIDING THAT THE
RIGHT TO KEEP AND BEAR ARMS
INCLUDES WITHOUT LIMITATION
THE POSSESSION AND USE OF
AMMUNITION, FIREARM
ACCESSORIES, AND FIREARM
COMPONENTS; AND PROVIDING
THAT THE RIGHT TO KEEP AND
BEAR ARMS IS A NATURAL,
FUNDAMENTAL, AND INDIVIDUAL
RIGHT THAT SHALL NOT BE
INFRINGED.
FOR ISSUE NO. 2
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 2
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 3
(Popular Name)
A Constitutional Amendment
Concerning Economic Development
in the State of Arkansas; and
Authorizing the General Assembly to
Provide for the Creation of Economic
Development Districts Within Cities,
Counties, or Cooperative Areas to
Promote Economic Development
Within the Economic Development
District.
(Ballot Title)
AN AMENDMENT TO THE
ARKANSAS CONSTITUTION
CONCERNING ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT IN THE STATE OF
ARKANSAS; AND AUTHORIZING
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY TO
PROVIDE FOR THE CREATION OF
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICTS WITHIN CITIES,
COUNTIES, OR COOPERATIVE
AREAS TO PROMOTE ECONOMIC
DEVELOPMENT WITHIN THE
ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT
DISTRICT.
FOR ISSUE NO. 3
AGAINST ISSUE NO. 3
STATEWIDE ISSUES
CONSTITUTIONAL ADMENDMENT
(OR OTHER MEASURE)
REFFERRED TO THE PEOPLE BY
THE GENERAL ASSEMBLY
ISSUE NO. 4
(Ballot Title)
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025.
(Ballot Question)
Shall the Arkansas Natural
Resources Commission be
authorized to issue general obligation
bonds under the authority of the
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
Financing Act of 2025, for the
financing and refinancing of the
development of water, waste
disposal, water pollution control,
abatement and prevention, drainage,
irrigation, flood control, and wetlands
and aquatic resources projects to
serve the citizens of the State of
Arkansas, in total principal amount
not to exceed five hundred million
dollars ($500,000,000), with no more
than one hundred sixty-five million
dollars ($165,000,000) in total
principal amount of such bonds to be
issued to finance and refinance the
development of irrigation facilities, in
series from time to time in principal
amounts not to exceed, without prior
approval of the General Assembly,
sixty million dollars ($60,000,000) in
any two-year period beginning on July
1 of each odd-numbered year, which
bonds shall be secured by a pledge of
the full faith and credit of the State of
Arkansas?
FOR Issuance of State of Arkansas
Water, Waste Disposal, and
Pollution Abatement Facilities general
obligation bonds.
AGAINST Issuance of State of
Arkansas Water, Waste Disposal,
and Pollution Abatement Facilities
general obligation bonds.

COUNTY ISSUES
ISSUE INITIATED BY PETITION OF
THE PEOPLE
ISSUE NO. 5
LIBRARY MILLAGE
VOTE FOR or AGAINST the levy of a
nine tenths of one (.9) mill ad valorem
tax on the assessed value of all
taxable real and personal property
within the County of Garland,
Arkansas, with the nine tenths of one
(.9) mill tax to be pledged as a
continuing debt service tax to the
repayment of an issue or issues of
bonds not to exceed Thirty-Four
Million Eight Hundred Thousand and
NO/100 Dollars ($34,800,000) in
aggregate principal amount, to
finance the acquiring, constructing
and equipping of land and additional
capital improvements for the public
libraries owned and operated by the
Garland County Library Board and to
authorize the issuance of the bonds
on such terms and conditions as shall
be approved by the County.
FOR .9 MILL TAX
AGAINST .9 MILL TAX
COUNTY ISSUES
MEASURE REFERRED TO THE
PEOPLE BY THE QUORUM COURT
ISSUE NO. 6
0.625% COUNTY ROAD TAX
VOTE FOR REFERRED
ORDINANCE NO. O-26-07 or
AGAINST REFERRED ORDINANCE
NO. O-26-07. COUNTY-WIDE
0.625% SALES AND USE TAX
The continuation of the levy of
0.625% local sales and use Tax
within the County to finance all or a
portion of the costs of improvements
to existing, roads, streets, and related
structures, including particularly,
without limitation, overpasses,
underpasses, bridges and sidewalks,
and any necessary land, easements,
rights of way and related intersection
improvements, drainage and traffic
control devices and improvements
therefor (collectively, the
"Improvements"). If the question is
approved, there will be a continuation
of the existing levy within the County
of a 0.625% sales and use tax, the
net collections of which after the
State of Arkansas deducts its
administrative charges and required
rebates will be divided per capita
among the County and the
Municipalities within in the County
pursuant to state law. The term of the
sales and use tax levy shall be July 1,
2027, through June 30, 2032.
FOR ROAD TAX
AGAINST ROAD TAX
Review
